## 1. Target, inputs, and retained order

**Goal.** Obtain the fermionic part of our gauged LOG density for the future theory $`\ell_{\rm tot}=\ell_{2\partial}+\lambda_{\log}\ell_{\log}`$. The result contains every explicit bilinear generated by the calculated LOG multiplet and the complete correction from the implicit fermions in its supercovariant objects. Following the specified presentation, those implicit corrections are retained as differences from the corresponding bosonic values. No elementary-connection expansion or spectrum is claimed here.

**Starting point.** The inputs are the four calculated, once-projected, gauge-evaluated polynomials in Step 7, §§6.1–6.4. Their un-gauged origin and convention bridge are the canonical multiplet, CTF-01–CTF-04. We use the corrected active inputs under `sources/step7/supercovariant/`, covered by [the retained-order certificate](../../../../../log/action/README.md), lock `GAUGED-LOG-TWO-FERMI-CERTIFIED-v1`. Archived connection-expanded expressions are not input.

The calculation below begins with those known components; it does not repeat their six-spinor-derivative construction. It performs the new BF contractions. Section 7 gives every contracted sector, §8 writes both action contributions of each of the 94 spinor summands, and §11 collects the complete result. Consecutive displays under one sum heading continue that same sum. There are two summands per row, rather than a separate line for every factor or term.

| Required relation | Located reference in this project | Use here |
| --- | --- | --- |
| LOG primary and its descendants | S23 (3.24)–(3.26); cached master `CONVENTIONS.md`; calculated Steps 2–5 | Provenance of the four input slots |
| Full BF density and current | S23 (3.2), (3.27), component bridge LOG-S1-16; Step 7 (SC7-50)–(SC7-52) | Outer action coefficients, including the two current completions |
| Composite substitutions | Master GG02–GG04, GC07–GC09; Step 7 (SC7-07)–(SC7-19) | Already applied before gauge evaluation in the input |
| Full derivative and constraints | Fermionic Weyl calculation GC14–GC24; Step 7 (SC7-01)–(SC7-06), (SC7D-02), (SC7D-06)–(SC7D-07) | Interpret every surviving descendant and curvature |
| Clifford, Majorana, and doublet rules | Canonical multiplet (CTF-02), (CTF-03), (CTF-10); scoped convention lock | Canonicalize the new BF contractions |
| Exact bosonic restriction and boundary | Previous bosonic derivation, §§5, 11–13; comparison BC-01–BC-11 | Subtract the bosonic body in the identical BF representative |

**A1.** Work classically in five dimensions, off shell, with no field equation or auxiliary elimination. **A2.** Use only $`\sigma=1`$, $`b_\mu=0`$, dilatino $`\psi^i=0`$, and $`L_c^{ij}=\delta^{ij}L/\sqrt2`$ on the local nonzero-$`L`$ patch. The gravitino $`\psi_\mu^i`$ and independent compensator spinor $`\varphi_c^i`$ remain. **A3.** Retain terms of total physical fermion degree two and label degree four and above “high fermi terms.” This is a formal Grassmann-degree truncation, not a small-$`g`$ approximation. **A4.** Preserve all full covariant derivatives and their order, and evaluate the gauge only after differentiation. **A5.** No integration by parts is performed in the new fermionic assembly. The bosonic boundary relation is displayed separately. **A6.** Do not impose a bosonic background, set fluxes to zero, or use an auxiliary equation. Fourier, state-normalization, and renormalization conventions do not enter this local classical calculation.

The later two-derivative input remains our previous calculation [file 05](../../../../../common/two_derivative_action/details/construction.md), lock `2DER-GDW-P-PSI0-OZKAN1608-QUADRATIC-FERMION-v2`. No replacement action is imported from a source paper in this work.

## 2. Conventions and full derivatives

<!-- ASSERT_CONVENTION: natural_units=hbar=c=1, metric_signature=(-,+,+,+,+), gamma_volume=+i, bar=linear Majorana, derivative_order=preserved, coupling_convention=ell_tot=ell_2der+lambda_LOG*ell_LOG, regularization_scheme=classical-not-applicable -->

We inherit [the master conventions](../../references/LOG_MASTER.md), [the LOG conventions](../../../../../log/action/details/component_construction/results/gauged_full_fermionic_log/LOG_CONVENTIONS.md), and the scoped lock. Indices are $`a,b=0,\ldots,4`$, $`\alpha=1,\ldots,4`$, $`i,j=1,2`$. The symplectic-Majorana spinors and their covariant derivatives are Grassmann odd; the Majorana bar used in the algebra is linear.

```math
\eta_{ab}=\mathop{\mathrm{diag}}\nolimits(-,+,+,+,+),\quad \{\gamma^a,\gamma^b\}=2\eta^{ab},\quad \gamma^{a_1\cdots a_p}=\gamma^{[a_1}\cdots\gamma^{a_p]},\quad \gamma^{01234}=i,\quad \epsilon^{01234}=1,\quad \epsilon_{01234}=-1.\tag{FL-01}
```

Brackets have unit weight. The component curvature convention is $`R_{\mu\nu}{}^{ab}=2\partial_{[\mu}\omega_{\nu]}{}^{ab}+2\omega_{[\mu}{}^{ac}\omega_{\nu]c}{}^b`$, with $`R_{\mu\nu}=R_{\mu\rho}{}^{ab}e_b{}^\rho e_{\nu a}`$. A hat denotes the full supercovariant expression, and $`\widehat C_{abcd}=\widehat R(M)_{abcd}`$; it is not the torsion-free Weyl tensor except on the bosonic body.

```math
\bar u=u^T\mathsf C,\qquad \mathsf C^T=-\mathsf C,\qquad \mathsf C^2=-1,\qquad (\gamma^a)^T=\mathsf C\gamma^a\mathsf C^{-1},\qquad v_i=v^j\varepsilon_{ji},\quad v^i=\varepsilon^{ij}v_j,\quad \varepsilon_{12}=\varepsilon^{12}=1.\tag{FL-02}
```

In this algebra $`\overline{iu}=i\bar u`$. This linear operation must not be replaced by complex conjugation when collecting a coefficient. The indexed spinor tensor is $`(\gamma^{a_1\cdots a_p})^{\alpha\beta}=-(\mathsf C\gamma^{a_1\cdots a_p})_{\alpha\beta}`$; the matrix kernel in a Majorana bilinear is $`+\mathsf C\gamma^{a_1\cdots a_p}`$. This distinction fixes the spinor sign in the BF contraction. No fermionic functional variation is taken here, so no left/right functional-derivative convention is needed.

The derivative is exactly the full derivative used in the fermionic Weyl-squared work:

```math
\mathcal D_\mu\Phi=\partial_\mu\Phi-\delta_D(b_\mu)\Phi-\delta_M(\omega_\mu)\Phi-\delta_U(V_\mu)\Phi-\delta_Q(\psi_\mu)\Phi-\delta_S(\phi_\mu)\Phi-\delta_K(f_\mu)\Phi,\qquad \mathcal D_a=e_a{}^\mu\mathcal D_\mu,\qquad \Box^c\Phi=\mathcal D^a\mathcal D_a\Phi.\tag{FL-03}
```

Every occurrence $`\mathcal D_{a_1}\cdots\mathcal D_{a_n}\Phi`$ below means $`(\mathcal D_{a_1}\cdots\mathcal D_{a_n}\Phi)|_{\rm gauge}`$. The generators act on the unfixed descendant, including its tangent indices. The superscript $`c`$ on the box means conformal, not the independent compensator label. The descendants of $`\sigma`$ and $`\psi^i`$ remain meaningful after those fields are gauge fixed.

| Field or expression | Meaning | Engineering dimension after gauge fixing |
| --- | --- | ---: |
| $`C_a, L_c^{ij},L`$ | Graviphoton and independent compensator triplet/scalar | $`0`$ |
| $`g,N_c,E_c^a,\widehat G_{ab},\widehat H_{ab}`$ | Gauging, independent compensator slots, and supercovariant strengths | $`1`$ |
| $`\psi_a^i,\varphi_c^i,\psi^i`$ | Gravitino, compensator spinor, and defining dilatino before evaluating its derivatives | $`1/2`$ |
| $`\widehat C,\widehat R(V),f,D`$ | Curvatures and dependent bosonic composites | $`2`$ |
| $`\widehat R(Q),\phi_a,\chi,\mathcal D_a\psi`$ | Odd curvatures, connections, and descendants | $`3/2`$ |
| $`L_{\log},\varphi_{\log},N_{\log},E_{\log}`$ | Calculated LOG slots | $`3,7/2,4,4`$ |
| $`\lambda_{\log}`$ | Coefficient of this density | $`-2`$ |

We write $`\widehat G^2=\widehat G_{ab}\widehat G^{ab}`$ and $`L_c^2=L_c^{ij}L_{cij}=L^2`$. All $`c`$ subscripts on $`\varphi_c,N_c,L_c,E_c`$ label the independent compensator. The letters on their Lorentz indices remain ordinary tensor indices.

## 3. Gauge values and conventional constraints

The component projection is already included: $`L_{\log}=-2H|`$, $`\varphi_{\log}=-2i\varphi_G|`$, $`N_{\log}=-F_G|`$, $`E_{\log}=\mathcal H_G|`$. We insert these components directly into BF. No second projection factor appears.

From the gauged map (SC7-07), evaluate only undifferentiated gauge-fixed fields:

```math
Y^{ij}=\frac i{4\sigma}\bar\psi^{(i}\psi^{j)}-\frac g{2\sigma}L_c^{ij}\ \longrightarrow\ -\frac g2 L_c^{ij},\qquad T_{ab}=\frac18(\widehat G_{ab}-2\widehat H_{ab}),\qquad \chi^i=\frac i8\gamma^a\mathcal D_a\psi^i+\frac g8\varphi_c^i.\tag{FL-04}
```

In the scalar map, $`-\widehat G^2/16-26T^2/3+2\widehat G\cdot T`$ gives coefficients $`-1/16-13/96+1/4=5/96`$ for $`\widehat G^2`$, $`13/24-1/2=1/24`$ for $`\widehat G\cdot\widehat H`$, and $`-13/24`$ for $`\widehat H^2`$. Therefore

```math
D=\frac14\Box^c\sigma+\frac5{96}\widehat G^2+\frac1{24}\widehat G_{ab}\widehat H^{ab}-\frac{13}{24}\widehat H^2+\frac g4N_c+\frac{g^2}{16}L_c^{ij}L_{cij}.\tag{FL-05}
```

These substitutions and their ordered derivatives are already in the input polynomials. Differentiating their gauge-only values a second time would lose terms. For example, differentiating the unfixed $`Y`$ first gives the following second derivative on the gauge slice:

```math
\left.\mathcal D_a\mathcal D_bY^{ij}\right|=-\frac g2\mathcal D_a\mathcal D_bL_c^{ij}+\frac g2L_c^{ij}\mathcal D_a\mathcal D_b\sigma+\frac i4\left(\overline{\mathcal D_b\psi^{(i}}\mathcal D_a\psi^{j)}+\overline{\mathcal D_a\psi^{(i}}\mathcal D_b\psi^{j)}\right).\tag{FL-06}
```

The $`+g/2`$ arises from $`\mathcal D_a\mathcal D_b\sigma^{-1}|=-\mathcal D_a\mathcal D_b\sigma|`$ multiplying $`-g/2`$. Both bilinears follow from the ordinary even Leibniz rule on $`\bar\psi^{(i}\psi^{j)}`$; undifferentiated dilatinos vanish only after differentiation. Their metric traces coincide without exchanging odd factors. This illustrates why descendants survive and supplies a direct check on the inherited triplet.

The constraints and their solutions are (SC7-06):

```math
\begin{aligned}
R(P)&=0,\qquad \gamma^a\widehat R(Q)_{ab}^i=0,\qquad \widehat R(M)_{acb}{}^c=0,\\
\omega_\mu{}^{ab}&=\omega_\mu{}^{ab}(e)+\frac12\bar\psi^{[a}\gamma^{b]}\psi_\mu+\frac14\bar\psi^a\gamma_\mu\psi^b,\qquad \phi_a^i=\frac i3\gamma^b\widehat R'(Q)_{ab}^i-\frac i{24}\gamma_a\gamma^{bc}\widehat R'(Q)_{bc}^i,\\
f_{ab}&=-\frac16\mathcal R_{ab}+\frac1{48}\eta_{ab}\mathcal R,\qquad f^a{}_a=-\frac1{16}\mathcal R,\qquad \mathcal R_{\mu\nu}=\widehat R'(M)_{\mu\rho}{}^{ab}e_b{}^\rho e_{\nu a}.
\end{aligned}\tag{FL-07}
```

The primed curvatures omit their S or K connection, respectively: $`\widehat R'(Q)_{ab}=\widehat R(Q)_{ab}+2i\gamma_{[a}\phi_{b]}`$ and $`\widehat R'(M)_{\mu\nu}{}^{ab}=\widehat R(M)_{\mu\nu}{}^{ab}-8f_{[\mu}{}^{[a}e_{\nu]}{}^{b]}`$. Their remaining terms have the full project definitions. In particular, $`\mathcal R_{ab}`$ and $`f_{ab}`$ keep their ordered indices and are not made symmetric at two fermions.

To check the spinor constraint, let $`v_b=\gamma^a\widehat R'(Q)_{ab}`$ and $`A=\gamma^{ab}\widehat R'(Q)_{ab}`$. Antisymmetry rewrites the solution as $`\phi_b=-iv_b/3-i\gamma_bA/24`$. Then $`\gamma^bv_b=-A`$ and $`\gamma^b\gamma_b=5`$ give $`\gamma^b\phi_b=iA/8`$. Contract the full curvature:

```math
\gamma^a\widehat R(Q)_{ab}=v_b-i\gamma^a\gamma_a\phi_b+i\gamma^a\gamma_b\phi_a=v_b-3i\phi_b-i\gamma_b\gamma^a\phi_a=v_b-\left(v_b+\frac18\gamma_bA\right)+\frac18\gamma_bA=0.\tag{FL-08}
```

For the Lorentz constraint the trace equation is $`0=\mathcal R_{ab}+6f_{ab}+2\eta_{ab}f^c{}_c`$. Its trace is $`0=\mathcal R+16f^a{}_a`$; substituting it back gives (FL-07) with no symmetry assumption on $`ab`$.

The gauge-evaluated derivative relations used to interpret the final answer are

```math
\begin{aligned}
\mathcal D_a\sigma|&=0,\qquad \mathcal D_a\psi^i|=\frac18\gamma^{bc}(\widehat G_{bc}+2\widehat H_{bc})\psi_a^i-\frac g2L_c^{ij}\psi_{aj}-\phi_a^i,\\
\mathcal D_a\mathcal D_b\sigma|&=2f_{ab}-\frac i2\bar\psi_a\mathcal D_b\psi-2\bar\psi_a\gamma_b\chi,\qquad \Box^c\sigma|=-\frac18\mathcal R-\frac i2\bar\psi_a\mathcal D^a\psi-\frac i4\bar\psi_a\gamma^a\gamma^b\mathcal D_b\psi-\frac g4\bar\psi_a\gamma^a\varphi_c,\\
\mathcal D_aL_c^{ij}|&=\frac{\delta^{ij}}{\sqrt2}\partial_aL+\frac L{\sqrt2}\left(V'_a{}^i{}_k\delta^{jk}+V'_a{}^j{}_k\delta^{ik}\right)-i\bar\psi_a^{(i}\varphi_c^{j)}.
\end{aligned}\tag{FL-09}
```

The box follows by contracting the Hessian, then inserting $`f^a{}_a`$ and $`\chi`$ from (FL-04), (FL-07). Thus its scalar-curvature sign is negative: $`\Box^c\sigma|_0=-R/8`$. These are interpretation identities; we do not replace the full derivatives inside the final action. The spinor Hessian and spinor box retain (SC7D-12)–(SC7D-14) in the same input file. Higher ordered derivatives likewise retain their unfixed transformation laws, rather than being ordinary derivatives of (FL-09).

## 4. Derive the seven BF contributions

The gauge-unfixed component density is

```math
\ell_{\rm BF}=Y^{ij}L_{\log ij}+i\bar\lambda\varphi_{\log}-\frac12\bar\psi_a^i\gamma^a\lambda^jL_{\log ij}+C_a\left(E_{\log}^a+\frac i2\bar\psi_b\gamma^{ba}\varphi_{\log}+\frac14\bar\psi_b^i\gamma^{abc}\psi_c^jL_{\log ij}\right)+\sigma\left(N_{\log}+\frac12\bar\psi_a\gamma^a\varphi_{\log}+\frac i4\bar\psi_a^i\gamma^{ab}\psi_b^jL_{\log ij}\right).\tag{FL-10}
```

Identify the defining vector with $`(\sigma,C_\mu,\psi^i,Y^{ij})`$. The two terms containing an undifferentiated $`\lambda^i=\psi^i`$ vanish on the gauge. Set $`\sigma=1`$ and $`Y^{ij}=-gL_c^{ij}/2`$. This leaves

```math
\ell_{\rm BF}=N_{\log}-\frac g2L_c^{ij}L_{\log ij}+C_aE_{\log}^a+\frac12\bar\psi_a\gamma^a\varphi_{\log}+\frac i2C_a\bar\psi_b\gamma^{ba}\varphi_{\log}+\frac i4\bar\psi_a^i\gamma^{ab}\psi_b^jL_{\log ij}+\frac14C_a\bar\psi_b^i\gamma^{abc}\psi_c^jL_{\log ij}.\tag{FL-11}
```

Here and in this section equality is through the retained order when truncated LOG slots are used. The last two $`C_a`$ couplings are part of the ordinary current $`P_{\log}^a`$. Replacing $`P_{\log}^a`$ by $`E_{\log}^a`$ would omit them. The independent compensator supplies $`L_c`$ and the gauged composites; it is not $`L_{\log}`$.

Use the input notation $`[0],[1],[2]`$ for the number of **written** odd fields before expanding a supercovariant expression. The scalar, triplet and vector have $`[0]`$ and $`[2]`$ parts; the needed spinor has $`[1]`$. Substitution into each of the seven terms of (FL-11) gives the following complete ledger.

| Contribution | Inserted expression | Dimension | Canonical monomials |
| --- | --- | --- | ---: |
| $`\mathcal F_N`$ | $`N_{\log}[2]`$ | $`4`$ | 61 |
| $`\mathcal F_L`$ | $`-gL_c^{ij}L_{\log ij}[2]/2`$ | $`1+0+3`$ | 4 |
| $`\mathcal F_E`$ | $`C_aE_{\log}^a[2]`$ | $`0+4`$ | 157 |
| $`\mathcal F_{\mathrm{psiphi}}`$ | $`\bar\psi_a\gamma^a\varphi_{\log}[1]/2`$ | $`1/2+7/2`$ | 194 |
| $`\mathcal F_{\mathrm{Cpsiphi}}`$ | $`iC_a\bar\psi_b\gamma^{ba}\varphi_{\log}[1]/2`$ | $`0+1/2+7/2`$ | 406 |
| $`\mathcal F_{\mathrm{psipsiL}}`$ | $`i\bar\psi_a^i\gamma^{ab}\psi_b^jL_{\log ij}[0]/4`$ | $`1+3`$ | 8 |
| $`\mathcal F_{\mathrm{CpsipsiL}}`$ | $`C_a\bar\psi_b^i\gamma^{abc}\psi_c^jL_{\log ij}[0]/4`$ | $`0+1+3`$ | 8 |

No $`L_{\log}[2]`$ belongs in the last two terms: it would produce at least four physical fermions. The explicit sum is

```math
\mathcal F_{\rm explicit}=\mathcal F_N+\mathcal F_L+\mathcal F_E+\mathcal F_{\mathrm{psiphi}}+\mathcal F_{\mathrm{Cpsiphi}}+\mathcal F_{\mathrm{psipsiL}}+\mathcal F_{\mathrm{CpsipsiL}},\qquad \mathcal B=N_{\log}[0]-\frac g2L_c^{ij}L_{\log ij}[0]+C_aE_{\log}^a[0].\tag{FL-12}
```

$`\mathcal B`$ is a zero-**explicit**-fermion polynomial. It is still full supercovariant and is not the bosonic action. Section 6 accounts for its fermions. The individual sectors have 838 terms in total; collecting their sum leaves 835. The three pieces of $`\mathcal B`$ have $`80+8+216=304`$ terms before collection and 297 afterwards.

### 4.1 Triplet multiplication, including its signs

The complete triplet input needed here is

```math
\begin{aligned}
L_{\log}^{ij}[0]={}&-\frac{1}{3}\widehat R(V){}^{a}{}^{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{a}{}_{b} -\frac{4}{3}\widehat H{}^{a}{}^{b}\widehat R(V){}_{a}{}_{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}\\
&-gL_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\big(\Box^c \sigma\big) -\frac{1}{2}gL_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G^2\\
&-g^{3}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}L_c{}^{k}{}^{l}L_c{}_{k}{}_{l} +6g\widehat H{}^{a}{}^{b}\widehat H{}_{a}{}_{b}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\\
&-3N_cg^{2}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j} -g\big(\Box^c L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\big)
\end{aligned}
\tag{FL-L0}
```

The explicit-bilinear part is

```math
\begin{aligned}
L_{\log}^{ij}[2]={}&-\frac{5i}{6}\overline{\big(\mathcal D_{a}\psi{}^{i}\big)}\gamma{}^{a}{}^{b}\big(\mathcal D_{b}\psi{}^{j}\big) +\frac{2i}{3}\overline{\big(\mathcal D^{a}\psi{}^{i}\big)}\big(\mathcal D_{a}\psi{}^{j}\big)\\
&-\frac{11}{6}g\overline{\varphi_c{}^{j}}\gamma{}^{a}\big(\mathcal D_{a}\psi{}^{i}\big) -\frac{11}{6}g\overline{\varphi_c{}^{i}}\gamma{}^{a}\big(\mathcal D_{a}\psi{}^{j}\big)\\
&+\frac{17i}{6}g^{2}\overline{\varphi_c{}^{i}}\varphi_c{}^{j}
\end{aligned}
\tag{FL-L2}
```

Right lowering gives $`L_{\log ij}=\varepsilon_{ki}\varepsilon_{lj}L_{\log}^{kl}`$. Multiply each of the five bilinears by $`-gL_c^{ij}/2`$. The coefficient products are $`(-g/2)(-5i/6)=5ig/12`$, $`(-g/2)(2i/3)=-ig/3`$, $`(-g/2)(-11g/6)=11g^2/12`$ twice, and $`(-g/2)(17ig^2/6)=-17ig^3/12`$. In the two mixed terms relabel the dummy doublet pair $`i\leftrightarrow j`$ in one term. Since $`L_c^{ij}=L_c^{ji}`$ and no two spinors move past one another, their coefficients add to $`11g^2/6`$. The resulting four-term expression is (FL-S2) below.

For the two-gravitino terms, multiply each of the eight terms in (FL-L0) by the BF factor in the ledger. For example $`-\widehat R(V)^{abij}\widehat G_{ab}/3`$ gives $`-i\widehat R(V)^{ab}{}_{ij}\widehat G_{ab}\bar\psi_c^i\gamma^{cd}\psi_d^j/12`$ from the scalar prefactor. The corresponding current term is $`-C_a\widehat R(V)^{de}{}_{ij}\widehat G_{de}\bar\psi_b^i\gamma^{abc}\psi_c^j/12`$. The rank-three identity (FL-16) turns its coefficient into $`+i/24`$ multiplying the epsilon–rank-two contraction. All eight terms of each product are displayed in §7.

The scalar contribution has outer coefficient $`+1`$, so each of its 61 input bilinears is included with this coefficient. The vector contribution has outer coefficient $`+C_a`$; its 157 terms acquire that contraction without differentiating $`C_a`$ or interchanging a derivative. Their full contracted forms are (FL-S1) and (FL-S3). Thus these two operations introduce neither a boundary term nor a Grassmann sign.

## 5. Canonicalize the spinor contractions

Each one-fermion summand has either no gamma matrix, one gamma, or one antisymmetrized pair. BF adds $`\gamma^a`$ or $`\gamma^{ba}`$ on its left. Consequently all new Clifford products have at most four indices. The needed reductions follow directly from (FL-01).

First, separate the anticommutator and commutator:

```math
\gamma^a\gamma^b=\frac12\{\gamma^a,\gamma^b\}+\frac12[\gamma^a,\gamma^b]=\eta^{ab}+\gamma^{ab}.\tag{FL-13}
```

Insert $`\gamma^{bc}=(\gamma^b\gamma^c-\gamma^c\gamma^b)/2`$, and use $`\gamma^a\gamma^b=-\gamma^b\gamma^a+2\eta^{ab}`$ to collect the fully antisymmetric part and the two contractions. This gives

```math
\gamma^a\gamma^{bc}=\gamma^{abc}+\eta^{ab}\gamma^c-\eta^{ac}\gamma^b,\qquad \gamma^{ab}\gamma^c=\gamma^{abc}+\eta^{bc}\gamma^a-\eta^{ac}\gamma^b.\tag{FL-14}
```

For the remaining product use $`\gamma^{ab}\gamma^{cd}=\gamma^{ab}(\gamma^c\gamma^d-\eta^{cd})`$. Applying the second relation of (FL-14) first gives $`(\gamma^{abc}+\eta^{bc}\gamma^a-\eta^{ac}\gamma^b)\gamma^d-\eta^{cd}\gamma^{ab}`$. The three-gamma term times $`\gamma^d`$ contributes $`\gamma^{abcd}+\eta^{cd}\gamma^{ab}-\eta^{bd}\gamma^{ac}+\eta^{ad}\gamma^{bc}`$. The $`\eta^{cd}\gamma^{ab}`$ terms cancel, leaving

```math
\gamma^{ab}\gamma^{cd}=\gamma^{abcd}+\eta^{bc}\gamma^{ad}-\eta^{ac}\gamma^{bd}-\eta^{bd}\gamma^{ac}+\eta^{ad}\gamma^{bc}+\eta^{bc}\eta^{ad}-\eta^{ac}\eta^{bd}.\tag{FL-15}
```

The volume convention and weighted antisymmetrization give

```math
\gamma^{abc}=-\frac i2\epsilon^{abcde}\gamma_{de},\qquad \gamma^{abcd}=i\epsilon^{abcde}\gamma_e.\tag{FL-16}
```

The factor $`1/2`$ in the first identity removes the two permutations of the contracted pair. For example $`\gamma^{012}=-i\gamma_{34}`$ since $`\epsilon^{01234}=1`$; multiplying $`\gamma^{012}\gamma^{34}=i`$ verifies its sign because $`(\gamma^{34})^2=-1`$. The second identity gives $`\gamma^{0123}=i\gamma_4`$, consistent with $`\gamma^{01234}=i`$. The explicit matrix verification checks all independent triples and quadruples, not just these examples.

When collecting a bilinear, preserve spinor order until its exchange sign is known. For independent odd spinors,

```math
u_\alpha(\mathsf C\Gamma)_{\alpha\beta}v_\beta=-v_\beta(\mathsf C\Gamma)_{\alpha\beta}u_\alpha=-v^T(\mathsf C\Gamma)^Tu.\tag{FL-17}
```

The transpose signs of $`\mathsf C\Gamma`$ are $`-1,-1,+1`$ for ranks $`0,1,2`$, respectively. Thus exchanging the two uncontracted doublet spinors gives $`+,+,-`$. A contracted doublet epsilon supplies one additional minus when its indices are exchanged. These signs, tensor antisymmetries, and ordinary dummy-index relabeling are the only spinor-order operations used in the new assembly. No four-spinor Fierz transformation is required.

### 5.1 Work a derivative term into both couplings

Take the actual input summand $`\varphi_{\log}^i[1]\supset(5i/6)(\Box^c\sigma)\gamma^b\mathcal D_b\psi^i`$. Its first BF contraction is

```math
\frac12\bar\psi_a\gamma^a\left[\frac{5i}{6}(\Box^c\sigma)\gamma^b\mathcal D_b\psi\right]=\frac{5i}{12}(\Box^c\sigma)\bar\psi_a(\gamma^{ab}+\eta^{ab})\mathcal D_b\psi=\frac{5i}{12}(\Box^c\sigma)\bar\psi_a\gamma^{ab}\mathcal D_b\psi+\frac{5i}{12}(\Box^c\sigma)\bar\psi^b\mathcal D_b\psi.\tag{FL-18}
```

The coefficient follows from $`(1/2)(5i/6)`$; the second equality is (FL-13), and the final term is metric contraction. The derivative stays on the dilatino descendant throughout. For the current contraction, $`i(5i)/12=-5/12`$ first gives

```math
\frac i2C_a\bar\psi_b\gamma^{ba}\left[\frac{5i}{6}(\Box^c\sigma)\gamma^c\mathcal D_c\psi\right]=-\frac5{12}C_a(\Box^c\sigma)\bar\psi_b\left(\gamma^{bac}+\eta^{ac}\gamma^b-\eta^{bc}\gamma^a\right)\mathcal D_c\psi.\tag{FL-19}
```

Apply (FL-16) to the first term and contract the metrics in the other two:

```math
\left.\frac i2C_a\bar\psi_b\gamma^{ba}\varphi_{\log}[1]\right|_{\text{chosen term}}=\frac{5i}{24}C_a(\Box^c\sigma)\epsilon^{bacde}\bar\psi_b\gamma_{de}\mathcal D_c\psi-\frac5{12}C_a(\Box^c\sigma)\bar\psi_b\gamma^b\mathcal D^a\psi+\frac5{12}C_a(\Box^c\sigma)\bar\psi_b\gamma^a\mathcal D^b\psi.\tag{FL-20}
```

For a gamma-free example, the actual term $`-g\widehat G^2\varphi_c^i/4`$ gives $`-g\widehat G^2\bar\psi_a\gamma^a\varphi_c/8`$ and $`-igC_a\widehat G^2\bar\psi_b\gamma^{ba}\varphi_c/8`$. No Clifford reduction is needed. The two BF kernels act on all other summands by the same displayed identities; §8 records their actual resulting contractions one by one, including every coefficient.

### 5.2 Collection and the meaning of canonical form

We contract epsilon and metric pairs, reduce all gamma products to ranks at most two, preserve the ordering of covariant derivatives, apply the conventional traces in (FL-07), and collect equal tensor contractions. A trace-free curvature obeys only the symmetries established in the input; full pair-exchange symmetry of $`\widehat C`$ and symmetry of $`f_{ab}`$ are not assumed. This canonical form is algebraic. It is not a minimal basis under integration by parts or all differential identities.

The sum of the separately canonicalized seven sectors agrees exactly with canonicalizing the full BF substitution at once. Every input term and every resulting term has two explicit odd fields and dimension four. The entire calculation is polynomial in $`g`$, including its ungauged limit.

## 6. Retain the implicit fermionic completion

This step is essential even though no connections are expanded in the final expression. Let $`t`$ multiply every elementary physical fermion, with the independent bosonic fields fixed. The full supercovariant expressions then have

```math
\mathcal B(t)=\mathcal B|_0+t^2\mathcal B^{(2)}+O(t^4),\qquad \mathcal F_{\rm explicit}(t)=t^2\mathcal F_{\rm explicit}^{(2)}+O(t^4).\tag{FL-21}
```

The bosonic body $`|_0`$ means all physical fermions set to zero **after** evaluating the ordered derivative and connection definitions. Therefore subtracting the same BF density at $`t=0`$ yields

```math
\ell_{\log,\rm ferm}^{(2)}=\left[\mathcal F_{\rm explicit}+\mathcal B-\mathcal B|_0\right]_{\le2},\qquad \ell_{\log,\rm ferm}=\ell_{\log,\rm ferm}^{(2)}+\text{high fermi terms}.\tag{FL-22}
```

The outer projection counts **physical** fermions. There is no zeroth-order term inside it. In an explicit bilinear its even coefficients may be evaluated at their bosonic values and its odd factors at linear order; writing their full covariant forms differs only by the displayed higher-fermion remainder.

Here is the ordinary product rule underlying every term of $`\mathcal B-\mathcal B|_0`$. If $`X=X_0+X_2+O(t^4)`$ and $`Y=Y_0+Y_2+O(t^4)`$ are even covariants, then

```math
XY-X_0Y_0=X_2Y_0+X_0Y_2+X_2Y_2,\qquad [XY-X_0Y_0]_{\le2}=X_2Y_0+X_0Y_2.\tag{FL-23}
```

For any written product of even covariants, exactly one factor contributes its bilinear difference:

```math
\left[\prod_{r=1}^n X_r-\prod_{r=1}^n X_r|_0\right]_{\le2}=\sum_{s=1}^n\left[X_s-X_s|_0\right]_{2}\prod_{r\ne s}X_r|_0.\tag{FL-24}
```

Each ordered derivative of a field is treated as the full covariant tensor shown, not differentiated after taking its bosonic restriction. This avoids dropping the Q/S/K actions. Section 11.1 prints every coefficient and contraction of the 297-term $`\mathcal B`$, so (FL-22) retains the correction to every one of its terms. None is silently classified as bosonic merely because no spinor is written.

For transparency the primitive strength differences on the gauge are

```math
\widehat G_{ab}-G_{ab}=\frac i2\bar\psi_{[a}\psi_{b]},\qquad \widehat H_{abc}-H_{abc}=-\frac34\bar\psi_{[a}\gamma_b\psi_{c]},\qquad \widehat H_{ab}-H_{ab}=\frac1{16}\epsilon_{abcde}\bar\psi^{[c}\gamma^d\psi^{e]}.\tag{FL-25}
```

The last coefficient is $`(-1/12)(-3/4)=1/16`$. Here $`G=dC`$ and $`H_3=dB+C\wedge G/2+gE_{c3}/2`$. Differences of differentiated strengths also contain the covariant connections and the differentiated unfixed dilatino terms; they are not just ordinary derivatives of (FL-25).

For example, the first term of $`\mathcal B`$ yields

```math
\left[\frac16\widehat R(V)_{ab}^{ij}\widehat R(V)^{ab}_{ij}-\frac16 V_{ab}^{ij}V^{ab}_{ij}\right]_{2}=\frac13V_{ab}^{ij}\left[\widehat R(V)^{ab}_{ij}-V^{ab}_{ij}\right]_{2}.\tag{FL-26}
```

The coefficient doubles because the two even factors have identical contracted slots. No spinor interchange occurs. Its explicit Chern-Simons partner yields

```math
\left[-\frac1{12}\epsilon^{abcde}C_a\big(\widehat R(V)_{bc}^{ij}\widehat R(V)_{de\,ij}-V_{bc}^{ij}V_{de\,ij}\big)\right]_{2}=-\frac16\epsilon^{abcde}C_aV_{bc}^{ij}\left[\widehat R(V)_{de\,ij}-V_{de\,ij}\right]_{2}.\tag{FL-27}
```

Here exchange of the two two-form index pairs in epsilon is even, giving the same factor of two. For the $`5(\Box^c\sigma)^2/6`$ term, use the actual bosonic box $`-R/8`$:

```math
\left[\frac56\big((\Box^c\sigma)^2-R^2/64\big)\right]_2=-\frac{5R}{24}\left[\Box^c\sigma+\frac R8\right]_2,\qquad \left[\Box^c\sigma+\frac R8\right]_2=-\frac18[\mathcal R-R]_2-\frac i2\bar\psi_a\mathcal D^a\psi-\frac i4\bar\psi_a\gamma^a\gamma^b\mathcal D_b\psi-\frac g4\bar\psi_a\gamma^a\varphi_c.\tag{FL-28}
```

Thus a term containing only the written scalar box supplies curvature and gravitino bilinears. Setting that box to zero would remove both bosonic curvature terms and fermionic terms.

The $`-3g^2(\mathcal D_aL_c^{ij})(\mathcal D^aL_{cij})/4`$ term of $`\mathcal B`$ gives, using (FL-09),

```math
\left[-\frac{3g^2}{4}\left((\mathcal D_aL_c^{ij})(\mathcal D^aL_{cij})-(\nabla_aL_c^{ij})(\nabla^aL_{cij})\right)\right]_2=\frac{3ig^2}{2}(\nabla^aL_{cij})\bar\psi_a^{(i}\varphi_c^{j)}.\tag{FL-29}
```

In this equation only, $`\nabla L_c`$ denotes the bosonic body of the full derivative, including its $`SU(2)`$ connection. The factor is $`(-3g^2/4)\times2\times(-i)`$. It does not vanish for constant $`L`$ unless the relevant connection contribution also vanishes.

Finally, for a term with an ordered derivative, such as $`\widehat G^{ab}\mathcal D_a\mathcal D^c\widehat G_{bc}`$, (FL-23) gives the bilinear correction

```math
[\widehat G^{ab}-G^{ab}]_2(\mathcal D_a\mathcal D^c\widehat G_{bc})|_0+G^{ab}\left[\mathcal D_a\mathcal D^c\widehat G_{bc}-(\mathcal D_a\mathcal D^c\widehat G_{bc})|_0\right]_2.\tag{FL-30}
```

The bosonic derivative value on the first factor includes the bosonic descendant connections; it is not assumed to equal an ordinary twice-differentiated two-form. Equations (FL-23)–(FL-30) explain exactly how the retained supercovariant-minus-bosonic presentation counts the hidden terms. Their full elementary bilinear expansion is intentionally deferred.

## 7. Seven separately collected contributions

These are the seven expressions calculated from the ledger in §4. The tags retain the sector numbering: S1 is the scalar, S2 the triplet contraction, S3 the vector contraction, S4–S5 the spinor couplings, and S6–S7 the two-gravitino couplings. Every expression uses the full derivatives of (FL-03). Their algebraic sum is collected once more in §11.2.

```math
&-\frac{i}{8}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat G{}_{e}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}^{e}{}^{i}}\gamma{}_{a}{}^{d}\big(\mathcal D^{g}\psi{}_{i}\big) -\frac{i}{8}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat G{}_{d}{}^{e}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{e}{}_{f}\overline{\psi{}^{d}{}^{i}}\big(\mathcal D^{f}\psi{}_{i}\big)\\
&+\frac{1}{4}gC{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{a}{}_{c}\overline{\psi{}^{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}\big(\mathcal D_{b}\psi{}_{j}\big) -\frac{1}{4}gC{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{a}{}_{b}\overline{\psi{}^{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}\big(\mathcal D_{c}\psi{}_{j}\big)\\
&-\frac{1}{4}gC{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{b}{}_{c}\overline{\psi{}^{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}\big(\mathcal D_{a}\psi{}_{j}\big) -\frac{i}{8}gC{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}^{d}{}^{e}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}\overline{\psi{}_{b}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}\big(\mathcal D^{f}\psi{}_{j}\big)\\
&-\frac{i}{8}gC{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}^{d}{}^{e}\epsilon{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}\overline{\psi{}^{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}_{a}{}^{b}\big(\mathcal D^{f}\psi{}_{j}\big) -\frac{i}{8}gC{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}^{c}{}^{d}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}\overline{\psi{}^{b}{}_{i}}\big(\mathcal D^{e}\psi{}_{j}\big)
\end{aligned}
\tag{FL-S5-11}
```

```math
\begin{aligned}
&+\frac{1}{8}g^{2}C{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}^{d}{}^{e}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}\overline{\varphi_c{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}\psi{}^{c}{}_{j} +\frac{i}{4}g^{2}C{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{b}{}_{c}\overline{\varphi_c{}_{i}}\gamma{}_{a}{}^{b}\psi{}^{c}{}_{j}\\
&+\frac{i}{4}g^{2}C{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{a}{}_{c}\overline{\varphi_c{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}\psi{}_{b}{}_{j} -\frac{i}{4}g^{2}C{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{a}{}_{b}\overline{\varphi_c{}_{i}}\psi{}^{b}{}_{j}\\
&+\frac{i}{12}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat G{}^{e}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{c}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}_{d}{}^{i}}\gamma{}_{b}{}^{d}\big(\mathcal D^{g}\psi{}_{i}\big) -\frac{i}{12}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat G{}^{e}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}^{d}{}^{i}}\gamma{}_{a}{}_{b}\big(\mathcal D^{g}\psi{}_{i}\big)\\
&-\frac{i}{12}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}_{a}{}^{b}\widehat G{}^{e}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{b}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}_{c}{}^{i}}\gamma{}^{c}{}^{d}\big(\mathcal D^{g}\psi{}_{i}\big) -\frac{i}{12}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}_{a}{}^{b}\widehat G{}^{d}{}^{e}\epsilon{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}\overline{\psi{}^{c}{}^{i}}\big(\mathcal D^{f}\psi{}_{i}\big)\\
&+\frac{i}{12}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat G{}^{e}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}_{b}{}^{i}}\gamma{}_{a}{}^{d}\big(\mathcal D^{g}\psi{}_{i}\big) -\frac{i}{12}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat G{}^{d}{}^{e}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}\overline{\psi{}_{b}{}^{i}}\big(\mathcal D^{f}\psi{}_{i}\big)
```

### Scalar-prefactor two-gravitino contraction

```math
\begin{aligned}
\mathcal F_{psipsiL}={}&-\frac{i}{12}\widehat R(V){}^{a}{}^{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{a}{}_{b}\overline{\psi{}_{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{c}{}^{d}\psi{}_{d}{}_{j} -\frac{i}{3}\widehat H{}^{a}{}^{b}\widehat R(V){}_{a}{}_{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}\overline{\psi{}_{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{c}{}^{d}\psi{}_{d}{}_{j}\\
&-\frac{i}{4}gL_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\big(\Box^c \sigma\big)\overline{\psi{}_{a}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{a}{}^{b}\psi{}_{b}{}_{j} -\frac{i}{8}gL_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G^2\overline{\psi{}_{a}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{a}{}^{b}\psi{}_{b}{}_{j}\\
&-\frac{i}{4}g^{3}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}L_c{}_{i}{}_{j}L_c{}^{k}{}^{l}\overline{\psi{}_{a}{}_{k}}\gamma{}^{a}{}^{b}\psi{}_{b}{}_{l} +\frac{3i}{2}g\widehat H{}^{a}{}^{b}\widehat H{}_{a}{}_{b}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\overline{\psi{}_{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{c}{}^{d}\psi{}_{d}{}_{j}\\
&-\frac{3i}{4}N_cg^{2}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\overline{\psi{}_{a}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{a}{}^{b}\psi{}_{b}{}_{j} -\frac{i}{4}g\big(\Box^c L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\big)\overline{\psi{}_{b}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}\psi{}_{c}{}_{j}
\end{aligned}
\tag{FL-S6}
```

### Current two-gravitino contraction

```math
\begin{aligned}
\mathcal F_{CpsipsiL}={}&\frac{i}{24}C{}^{a}\widehat R(V){}^{b}{}^{c}{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{b}{}_{c}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}^{f}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{d}{}^{e}\psi{}^{g}{}_{j} +\frac{i}{6}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat R(V){}_{b}{}_{c}{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}^{f}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{d}{}^{e}\psi{}^{g}{}_{j}\\
&+\frac{i}{8}gC{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\big(\Box^c \sigma\big)\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}\overline{\psi{}^{d}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}\psi{}^{e}{}_{j} +\frac{i}{16}gC{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}\widehat G^2\overline{\psi{}^{d}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}\psi{}^{e}{}_{j}\\
&+\frac{i}{8}g^{3}C{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}L_c{}_{i}{}_{j}L_c{}^{k}{}^{l}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}\overline{\psi{}^{d}{}_{k}}\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}\psi{}^{e}{}_{l} -\frac{3i}{4}gC{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat H{}_{b}{}_{c}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}^{f}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{d}{}^{e}\psi{}^{g}{}_{j}\\
&+\frac{3i}{8}N_cg^{2}C{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}\overline{\psi{}^{d}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}\psi{}^{e}{}_{j} +\frac{i}{8}gC{}^{a}\big(\Box^c L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\big)\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}\overline{\psi{}^{e}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{c}{}^{d}\psi{}^{f}{}_{j}
\end{aligned}
\tag{FL-S7}
```

## 8. Every spinor summand inserted into both BF couplings

The calculation here resolves the two matrix products for **each** of the 94 input spinor summands. The displayed summand labels enumerate the saved canonical input. They are equation locators, not new component fields. Each entry shows the original summand and its contributions to $`\bar\psi_a\gamma^a\varphi_{\log}/2`$ and $`iC_a\bar\psi_b\gamma^{ba}\varphi_{\log}/2`$. The lowering, coefficient multiplication, Clifford identities, and Grassmann collection are (FL-02), (FL-13)–(FL-17). No derivative moves across a field. Summing the first output of all entries gives (FL-S4), and summing the second gives (FL-S5), with exact zero coefficient residual in each comparison.

#### Spinor summand 1

```math
\begin{aligned}
\left.\varphi_{\log}^{i}[1]\right|_{\text{summand }1}={}&\frac{1}{6}\widehat R(V){}^{a}{}^{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}\gamma{}^{c}{}^{d}\big(\mathcal D^{e}\psi{}_{j}\big)
\end{aligned}
\tag{FL-P1}
```

Insert the summand, multiply its coefficient by the BF prefactor, and right-lower its doublet index. The actual matrix products before Clifford reduction are

```math
\begin{aligned}
\left.\frac12\bar\psi_a\gamma^a\varphi_{\log}[1]\right|_{\text{summand }1}={}&-\frac{1}{12}\widehat R(V){}^{a}{}^{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}\overline{\psi{}_{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{c}\gamma{}^{d}{}^{e}\big(\mathcal D^{f}\psi{}_{j}\big)
\end{aligned}
\tag{FL-PA1-input}
```

```math
\begin{aligned}
\left.\frac i2 C_a\bar\psi_b\gamma^{ba}\varphi_{\log}[1]\right|_{\text{summand }1}={}&\frac{i}{12}C{}^{a}\widehat R(V){}^{b}{}^{c}{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}_{d}{}_{i}}\gamma{}_{a}{}^{d}\gamma{}^{e}{}^{f}\big(\mathcal D^{g}\psi{}_{j}\big)
\end{aligned}
\tag{FL-PB1-input}
```

Apply (FL-13)–(FL-16) to these written adjacent gamma matrices, then collect using the tensor symmetries and the exchange sign (FL-17). The same contributions in the declared canonical basis are

```math
\begin{aligned}
\left.\frac12\bar\psi_a\gamma^a\varphi_{\log}[1]\right|_{\text{summand }1}={}&\frac{i}{3}\widehat R(V){}^{a}{}^{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}\overline{\psi{}_{b}{}_{i}}\gamma{}_{a}{}^{c}\big(\mathcal D_{c}\psi{}_{j}\big) -\frac{i}{6}\widehat R(V){}^{a}{}^{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}\overline{\psi{}^{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}_{a}{}_{b}\big(\mathcal D_{c}\psi{}_{j}\big)\\
&+\frac{1}{6}\widehat R(V){}^{a}{}^{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}\overline{\psi{}^{d}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{c}\big(\mathcal D^{e}\psi{}_{j}\big)
\end{aligned}
\tag{FL-PA1}
```

```math
\begin{aligned}
\left.\frac i2 C_a\bar\psi_b\gamma^{ba}\varphi_{\log}[1]\right|_{\text{summand }1}={}&\frac{1}{3}C{}^{a}\widehat R(V){}_{a}{}^{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}\overline{\psi{}_{b}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{c}\big(\mathcal D_{c}\psi{}_{j}\big) -\frac{1}{3}C{}^{a}\widehat R(V){}_{a}{}^{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}\overline{\psi{}^{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}_{b}\big(\mathcal D_{c}\psi{}_{j}\big)\\
&-\frac{1}{3}C{}^{a}\widehat R(V){}^{b}{}^{c}{}^{i}{}^{j}\overline{\psi{}_{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}_{b}\big(\mathcal D_{a}\psi{}_{j}\big) -\frac{i}{6}C{}^{a}\widehat R(V){}^{b}{}^{c}{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{e}{}_{f}\overline{\psi{}_{d}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{d}{}^{e}\big(\mathcal D^{f}\psi{}_{j}\big)\\
&-\frac{i}{6}C{}^{a}\widehat R(V){}^{b}{}^{c}{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}\overline{\psi{}^{e}{}_{i}}\gamma{}_{a}{}^{d}\big(\mathcal D^{f}\psi{}_{j}\big) -\frac{i}{6}C{}^{a}\widehat R(V){}^{b}{}^{c}{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}\overline{\psi{}^{d}{}_{i}}\big(\mathcal D^{e}\psi{}_{j}\big)
\end{aligned}
\tag{FL-PB1}
```

```math
\end{aligned}
```

## 9. Bosonic subtraction and boundary representative

The body in (FL-22) is defined locally by the same BF expression:

```math
\mathcal B|_0=N_{\log}|_0-\frac g2L_c^{ij}L_{\log ij}|_0+C_aE_{\log}^a|_0.\tag{FL-31}
```

The following bosonic components and antisymmetric potential are given by the established calculation BC-01–BC-04, not inferred by stripping hats. They provide an explicit evaluation of the subtraction. In this section $`V_{ab}^{ij}`$ is the bosonic $`SU(2)`$ curvature, $`R(M)`$ the bosonic trace-free Lorentz curvature, and $`\nabla`$ the bosonic derivative after the descendant connections have been evaluated. The temporary $`T,D,f`$ on the right are the **bosonic** composites

```math
T_{ab}=\frac18(G_{ab}-2H_{ab}),\qquad f_{ab}=-\frac16R_{ab}+\frac1{48}\eta_{ab}R,\qquad D=-\frac R{32}+\frac5{96}G^2+\frac1{24}G\cdot H-\frac{13}{24}H^2+\frac g4N_c+\frac{g^2}{16}L_c^2.\tag{FL-32}
```

Every derivative of these bosonic identities uses the ordinary even covariant product rule in this subsection. Unlike the full $`f`$, this bosonic $`f`$ is symmetric.

These expressions were freshly rebuilt from the original calculated full-covariant component polynomials, retaining the bosonic contributions of the connection terms before gauge fixing. Their terms were not obtained by differentiating the comparison action.

```math
\begin{aligned}
L_{\log}^{ij}|_0={}&\frac{16}{3}V{}^{abij}T{}_{ab}-12DgL_c{}^{ij}-V{}^{abij}G{}_{ab} \\
&-g\big(\nabla^2 L_c{}^{ij}\big)-2gL_c{}^{ij}f{}^{a}{}_{a}-8gL_c{}^{ij}T^2 \\
&+\frac{1}{4}gL_c{}^{ij}G^2-\frac{1}{4}g^3L_c{}^{ij}L_c{}^{kl}L_c{}_{kl}
\end{aligned}\tag{FL-B0-L}
```

```math
\begin{aligned}
N_{\log}|_0={}&\frac{1}{6}V{}^{abij}V{}_{abij}-\frac{88}{9}R(M){}^{abcd}T{}_{ab}T{}_{cd}-\frac{80}{9}R(M){}^{abcd}T{}_{ac}T{}_{bd} \\
&+\frac{10720}{27}(T^2)^{2}-\frac{256}{3}T{}^{ab}T{}^{cd}\big(\nabla^{e}T{}_{e}{}^{f}\big)\epsilon{}_{abcdf}+\frac{736}{9}DT^2 \\
&+\frac{3}{2}\big(\nabla^{a}R(M){}_{a}{}^{bcd}\big)T{}^{ef}\epsilon{}_{bcdef}+\big(\nabla^{a}R(M){}^{bcde}\big)T{}_{b}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{acdef}+\big(\nabla^{a}R(M){}^{bcde}\big)T{}_{a}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{bcdef} \\
&+\frac{512}{9}T{}^{ab}\big(\nabla_{a}\nabla^{c}T{}_{bc}\big)-\frac{512}{9}f{}^{ab}T{}_{a}{}^{c}T{}_{bc}-\frac{208}{3}T{}^{ab}\big(\nabla^2 T{}_{ab}\big) \\
&-\frac{944}{9}f{}^{a}{}_{a}T^2-\frac{128}{9}T{}^{ab}\big(\nabla^{c}\nabla_{a}T{}_{bc}\big)-\frac{8}{3}D^{2} \\
&+\frac{64}{3}\big(\nabla^{a}T{}^{bc}\big)\big(\nabla_{b}T{}_{ac}\big)-\frac{176}{3}\big(\nabla^{a}T{}^{bc}\big)\big(\nabla_{a}T{}_{bc}\big)+64\big(\nabla^{a}T{}_{a}{}^{b}\big)\big(\nabla^{c}T{}_{bc}\big) \\
&-3Dg^2L_c{}^{ij}L_c{}_{ij}-8Df{}^{a}{}_{a}-\frac{1}{2}gL_c{}^{ij}V{}^{ab}{}_{ij}G{}_{ab} \\
&+\frac{7}{6}R(M){}^{abcd}G{}_{ab}T{}_{cd}+\frac{1}{3}R(M){}^{abcd}G{}_{ac}T{}_{bd}+\frac{563}{36}G{}^{ab}T{}^{cd}\big(\nabla_{a}T{}^{ef}\big)\epsilon{}_{bcdef} \\
&-\frac{139}{18}G{}^{ab}T{}_{a}{}^{c}\big(\nabla^{d}T{}^{ef}\big)\epsilon{}_{bcdef}+\frac{38}{3}G{}^{ab}T{}^{cd}\big(\nabla^{e}T{}_{a}{}^{f}\big)\epsilon{}_{bcdef}-\frac{361}{36}G{}^{ab}T{}^{cd}\big(\nabla^{e}T{}_{e}{}^{f}\big)\epsilon{}_{abcdf} \\
&+\frac{89}{18}G{}^{ab}T{}^{cd}\big(\nabla^{e}T{}_{c}{}^{f}\big)\epsilon{}_{abdef}-\frac{25}{2}G{}^{ab}T{}^{cd}\big(\nabla_{c}T{}^{ef}\big)\epsilon{}_{abdef}-\frac{9}{32}\big(\nabla^{a}R(M){}_{a}{}^{bcd}\big)G{}^{ef}\epsilon{}_{bcdef} \\
&-\frac{3}{16}\big(\nabla^{a}R(M){}^{bcde}\big)G{}_{b}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{acdef}-\frac{3}{16}\big(\nabla^{a}R(M){}^{bcde}\big)G{}_{a}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{bcdef}+\frac{16}{3}G{}^{ab}\big(\nabla_{a}\nabla^{c}T{}_{bc}\big) \\
&+\frac{16}{3}f{}^{ab}G{}_{a}{}^{c}T{}_{bc}+8G{}^{ab}\big(\nabla^2 T{}_{ab}\big)+\frac{16}{3}f{}^{a}{}_{a}G{}^{bc}T{}_{bc} \\
&+\frac{8}{3}G{}^{ab}\big(\nabla^{c}\nabla_{a}T{}_{bc}\big)-\frac{1}{2}g^2L_c{}^{ij}\big(\nabla^2 L_c{}_{ij}\big)-\frac{1}{2}g^2L_c{}^{ij}L_c{}_{ij}f{}^{a}{}_{a} \\
&+2\big(\nabla^2 f{}^{b}{}_{b}\big)-6f{}^{ab}f{}_{ab}+8f{}^{a}{}_{a}f{}^{b}{}_{b} \\
&+6\big(\nabla^{a}G{}^{bc}\big)\big(\nabla_{a}T{}_{bc}\big)+\frac{8}{3}\big(\nabla^2 G{}^{bc}\big)T{}_{bc}-8\big(\nabla^{a}G{}_{a}{}^{b}\big)\big(\nabla^{c}T{}_{bc}\big) \\
&+4\big(\nabla^{a}G{}^{bc}\big)\big(\nabla_{b}T{}_{ac}\big)+\frac{32}{3}\big(\nabla^{a}\nabla^{b}G{}_{b}{}^{c}\big)T{}_{ac}+16\big(\nabla^{a}G{}_{a}{}^{b}\big)T{}^{cd}T{}^{ef}\epsilon{}_{bcdef} \\
&-4\big(\nabla^2 D\big)-2g^2L_c{}^{ij}L_c{}_{ij}T^2-\frac{1}{4}g^2\big(\nabla^{a}L_c{}^{ij}\big)\big(\nabla_{a}L_c{}_{ij}\big) \\
&+\frac{1}{8}R(M){}^{abcd}G{}_{ab}G{}_{cd}+\frac{1}{4}R(M){}^{abcd}G{}_{ac}G{}_{bd}+\frac{50}{3}G^2T^2 \\
&-64G{}^{ab}G{}_{a}{}^{c}T{}_{b}{}^{d}T{}_{cd}+2G{}^{ab}G{}^{cd}\big(\nabla^{e}T{}_{e}{}^{f}\big)\epsilon{}_{abcdf}+\frac{1}{2}G{}^{ab}\big(\nabla^2 G{}_{ab}\big) \\
&+\frac{3}{2}f{}^{a}{}_{a}G^2+\frac{25}{12}G{}^{ab}\big(\nabla_{a}G{}^{cd}\big)T{}^{ef}\epsilon{}_{bcdef}-\frac{17}{6}G{}^{ab}\big(\nabla^{c}G{}^{de}\big)T{}_{a}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{bcdef} \\
&-\frac{7}{3}G{}^{ab}\big(\nabla^{c}G{}_{a}{}^{d}\big)T{}^{ef}\epsilon{}_{bcdef}+\frac{35}{12}G{}^{ab}\big(\nabla^{c}G{}_{c}{}^{d}\big)T{}^{ef}\epsilon{}_{abdef}+\frac{11}{6}G{}^{ab}\big(\nabla^{c}G{}^{de}\big)T{}_{d}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{abcef} \\
&+2G{}^{ab}\big(\nabla^{c}G{}^{de}\big)T{}_{c}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{abdef}+\frac{1}{4}\big(\nabla^{a}G{}^{bc}\big)\big(\nabla_{a}G{}_{bc}\big)+\frac{3}{16}g^2L_c{}^{ij}L_c{}_{ij}G^2 \\
&-4G{}^{ab}T{}_{ab}G^2+DG^2-\frac{5}{32}g^4L_c{}^{ij}L_c{}_{ij}L_c{}^{kl}L_c{}_{kl} \\
&-\frac{1}{8}G{}^{ab}G{}^{cd}\big(\nabla^{e}G{}_{e}{}^{f}\big)\epsilon{}_{abcdf}-2f{}^{ab}G{}_{a}{}^{c}G{}_{bc}+8G{}^{ab}G{}_{a}{}^{c}G{}_{b}{}^{d}T{}_{cd} \\
&+\frac{3}{32}(G^2)^{2}-\frac{3}{8}G{}^{ab}G{}_{a}{}^{c}G{}_{b}{}^{d}G{}_{cd}+\frac{1}{8}g^4L_c{}^{ij}L_c{}_{i}{}^{k}L_c{}_{j}{}^{l}L_c{}_{kl}
\end{aligned}\tag{FL-B0-N}
```

### Bosonic vector in divergence form

The bosonic vector equals the following local expression, with no integration by parts:

```math
E_{\log}^{a}|_0=\nabla_b Q^{ab}-\frac1{12}\epsilon^{abcde}V_{bc}^{ij}V_{de\,ij},\qquad Q^{ab}=-Q^{ba}.\tag{FL-B0-E}
```

The potential is C24 (4.8d), (4.10c) after the field bridge and bosonic gauge evaluation. All 34 canonical terms are displayed below; its divergence was compared directly to the calculated vector. The equality uses the explicit pointwise identities in the report and the 116-row transcript in the linked bosonic derivation.

```math
\begin{aligned}
Q^{ab}={}&-\frac{64}{3}DT{}^{ab}+64T{}^{cd}\big(\nabla^{e}T{}_{e}{}^{f}\big)\epsilon{}^{ab}{}_{cdf}+\frac{64}{3}T{}^{cd}\big(\nabla_{c}T{}^{ef}\big)\epsilon{}^{ab}{}_{def} \\
&-\frac{128}{9}T{}^{ab}T^2-\frac{2048}{3}T{}^{ac}T{}^{bd}T{}_{cd}+8R(M){}^{abcd}T{}_{cd} \\
&+8\big(\nabla^2 T{}^{ab}\big)+64f{}^{c}{}_{c}T{}^{ab}-4DG{}^{ab} \\
&-4G{}^{cd}\big(\nabla_{c}T{}^{ef}\big)\epsilon{}^{ab}{}_{def}-8G{}^{cd}\big(\nabla^{e}T{}_{e}{}^{f}\big)\epsilon{}^{ab}{}_{cdf}-8\big(\nabla^{c}G{}_{c}{}^{d}\big)T{}^{ef}\epsilon{}^{ab}{}_{def} \\
&-4\big(\nabla^{c}G{}^{de}\big)T{}_{c}{}^{f}\epsilon{}^{ab}{}_{def}-\big(\nabla^2 G{}^{ab}\big)-6f{}^{c}{}_{c}G{}^{ab} \\
&-\frac{200}{3}G{}^{ab}T^2-128G{}^{bc}T{}^{ad}T{}_{cd}+128G{}^{ac}T{}^{bd}T{}_{cd} \\
&+gL_c{}^{ij}V{}^{ab}{}_{ij}-R(M){}^{abcd}G{}_{cd}+\frac{1}{4}G{}^{cd}\big(\nabla_{c}G{}^{ef}\big)\epsilon{}^{ab}{}_{def} \\
&+\frac{1}{2}G{}^{cd}\big(\nabla^{e}G{}_{e}{}^{f}\big)\epsilon{}^{ab}{}_{cdf}+4f{}^{bc}G{}^{a}{}_{c}-4f{}^{ac}G{}^{b}{}_{c} \\
&+8G{}^{bc}G{}_{c}{}^{d}T{}^{a}{}_{d}-8G{}^{ac}G{}_{c}{}^{d}T{}^{b}{}_{d}+4T{}^{ab}G^2 \\
&-8G{}^{ac}G{}^{bd}T{}_{cd}+8G{}^{ab}G{}^{cd}T{}_{cd}-\frac{1}{4}G{}^{ab}G^2 \\
&+G{}^{ac}G{}^{bd}G{}_{cd}-\frac{1}{4}g^2L_c{}^{ij}L_c{}_{ij}G{}^{ab}+16f{}^{ac}T{}^{b}{}_{c} \\
&-16f{}^{bc}T{}^{a}{}_{c}
\end{aligned}\tag{FL-B0-Q}
```

Antisymmetry of $`Q^{ab}`$ gives $`C_a\nabla_bQ^{ab}=\nabla_b(C_aQ^{ab})+G_{ab}Q^{ab}/2`$. The already derived comparison to the collected bosonic LOG density is

```math
\mathcal B|_0=\ell_{\log,\rm collected}^{(0)}+\nabla_b\left(C_aQ^{ab}+J^b\right).\tag{FL-33}
```

Here $`Q`$ has the substitutions (FL-32); the complete 16-term $`J^b`$ is displayed as BC-10 in the previous bosonic derivation, §11. Thus subtracting the collected bosonic action **without** its divergence from the original BF representative would leave an unwanted bosonic boundary term inside what was called “fermionic.” We use the local subtraction (FL-31). No boundary term is dropped in deriving (FL-22).

For the eventual integrated bulk action one may combine $`\ell_{\log,\rm collected}^{(0)}`$ with (FL-22), provided the explicitly stated bosonic divergence in (FL-33) is treated with the same boundary convention as in the bosonic note. A supersymmetric boundary action is outside the present calculation.

## 10. Verification and scope

**Exact assembly.** The reproducible assembly script contracts the current input components. It checks every output term for scalar index structure, engineering dimension four, and explicit fermion degree. Reapplying the declared canonicalization leaves all sectors unchanged. Canonicalizing the entire BF product at once minus the sum of the separately canonicalized sectors gives exactly zero. The [assembly record](07_assembly_checks.json) saves input hashes and term counts.

**Independent spinor-sign and contraction check.** The verification script evaluates the original components first, then contracts their arrays directly with ordinary $`4\times4`$ matrices $`\mathsf C\gamma^a`$, $`\mathsf C\gamma^{ba}`$, and $`\mathsf C\gamma^{abc}`$. Its right-lowering operation is $`L_{ij}=(\varepsilon^TL\varepsilon)_{ij}`$. This route does not use the symbolic outer BF constructor. Two independent Grassmann generators give the coefficient $`u_0^T\mathsf C\Gamma v_1-u_1^T\mathsf C\Gamma v_0`$. The symbolic route uses the dual rank-three kernel, while the independent route uses rank three directly.

All seven sectors and all three zero-explicit-fermion products agree for both $`g=0.41`$ and $`g=0`$, with maximum relative residual $`1.20\times10^{-15}`$. The total explicit sum residuals are $`6.77\times10^{-16}`$ and $`3.26\times10^{-16}`$. The exact matrix tests cover 480 entries of the Majorana kernel, four right-lowering entries, 160 Clifford identities, 16 duality identities, and 240 indexed-gamma placements. An additional 625 exact matrix products check (FL-15), and five check the transpose convention (FL-02). The hand example (FL-18) has zero residual; (FL-19)–(FL-20) agrees to $`4.50\times10^{-14}`$ in absolute units in the independently entered integer-field test. The gamma-trace projector agrees to $`1.80\times10^{-15}`$, and the Lorentz trace solution is tested with a nonsymmetric primed Ricci tensor, with residual $`1.12\times10^{-16}`$. Results are here and here.

**Complete coverage.** Each of the 94 spinor summands has two displayed BF contractions; the transcript sums agree exactly with the two independently assembled sectors. Section 11 contains all 835 collected explicit monomials and all 297 zero-explicit monomials generating the implicit completion. The [coverage record](08_derivation_coverage.json) records these checks. The ordinary product rule for the completion was additionally tested with exact integer dual-number products at every multiplication length present in $`\mathcal B`$.

**Bosonic and ungauged limits.** Setting all physical fermions to zero makes every explicit bilinear vanish, and $`\mathcal B-\mathcal B|_0=0`$ identically; the fermionic result is zero. The full action then gives precisely (FL-31), whose reduction to the previous collected bosonic action is (FL-33). At $`g=0`$ the complete $`\mathcal F_L`$ sector vanishes and all $`g`$-dependent monomials drop polynomially. Curvature and connection fermions remain, as they should for the ungauged higher-derivative invariant. No inverse power of $`g`$ is introduced.

**Constraints, reality, and supersymmetry.** The explicit projector calculation (FL-08) checks the $`R(Q)`$ gamma trace; (FL-07) checks the $`R(M)`$ trace without imposing a false full Ricci symmetry. Tensor and doublet indices are fully contracted in every new action term. The assembly preserves the locked Majorana/doublet conventions, so the real-density and closed-BF-superform properties are inherited from the cited retained-order certificate. We have not performed a new independent nonlinear SUSY variation, and arbitrary complex random test arrays do not establish physical spinor reality. The numerical checks test the new algebraic contractions. The derivative and curvature completion is retained in the exact covariant form specified in the conventions.

The inherited LOG constraint remains $`\mathcal D^aE_{\log a}=0`$ through the retained order, while the completed ordinary current obeys $`\partial_\mu(eP_{\log}^{\mu})=0`$ in its potential representation. Their relation is the full-current identity already certified in Step 7 (SC7-50), (SC7-53); this assembly keeps both of its explicit gravitino terms. Neither condition sets the independent compensator vector $`E_c^a`$ to zero.

**Reproduction.** In the project root, run the following with the local runtime used here:

```bash
python3 results/twoder_plus_log_spectrum/build_fermionic_log.py
python3 results/twoder_plus_log_spectrum/verify_fermionic_log.py
python3 results/twoder_plus_log_spectrum/write_fermionic_log.py
```

These action checks do not determine a background, fermionic Hessian, or mass spectrum. The Hessian requires variation of the full covariants and inclusion of the two-derivative density before applying the background equations.

## 11. Complete collected result

The two full polynomials below use only the existing gauged fields, full supercovariant curvatures, and ordered full derivatives. All algebraic Standard-Weyl composites have already been substituted. The correction $`\mathcal B-\mathcal B|_0`$ retains every implicit bilinear, with its bosonic subtraction specified explicitly in §9. The result therefore does not leave $`L_{\log},\varphi_{\log},N_{\log},E_{\log}`$ as unevaluated action slots.

```math
&+\frac{i}{4}g^{2}C{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{b}{}_{c}\overline{\varphi_c{}_{i}}\gamma{}_{a}{}^{b}\psi{}^{c}{}_{j} +\frac{i}{4}g^{2}C{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{a}{}_{c}\overline{\varphi_c{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}\psi{}_{b}{}_{j}\\
&-\frac{i}{4}g^{2}C{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{a}{}_{b}\overline{\varphi_c{}_{i}}\psi{}^{b}{}_{j} +\frac{i}{12}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat G{}^{e}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{c}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}_{d}{}^{i}}\gamma{}_{b}{}^{d}\big(\mathcal D^{g}\psi{}_{i}\big)\\
&-\frac{i}{12}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat G{}^{e}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}^{d}{}^{i}}\gamma{}_{a}{}_{b}\big(\mathcal D^{g}\psi{}_{i}\big) -\frac{i}{12}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}_{a}{}^{b}\widehat G{}^{e}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{b}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}_{c}{}^{i}}\gamma{}^{c}{}^{d}\big(\mathcal D^{g}\psi{}_{i}\big)\\
&-\frac{i}{12}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}_{a}{}^{b}\widehat G{}^{d}{}^{e}\epsilon{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}\overline{\psi{}^{c}{}^{i}}\big(\mathcal D^{f}\psi{}_{i}\big) +\frac{i}{12}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat G{}^{e}{}^{f}\epsilon{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}_{b}{}^{i}}\gamma{}_{a}{}^{d}\big(\mathcal D^{g}\psi{}_{i}\big)\\
&-\frac{i}{12}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat G{}^{d}{}^{e}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}\overline{\psi{}_{b}{}^{i}}\big(\mathcal D^{f}\psi{}_{i}\big) -\frac{i}{12}\widehat R(V){}^{a}{}^{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{a}{}_{b}\overline{\psi{}_{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{c}{}^{d}\psi{}_{d}{}_{j}\\
&-\frac{i}{3}\widehat H{}^{a}{}^{b}\widehat R(V){}_{a}{}_{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}\overline{\psi{}_{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{c}{}^{d}\psi{}_{d}{}_{j} -\frac{i}{4}gL_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\big(\Box^c \sigma\big)\overline{\psi{}_{a}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{a}{}^{b}\psi{}_{b}{}_{j}\\
&-\frac{i}{8}gL_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G^2\overline{\psi{}_{a}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{a}{}^{b}\psi{}_{b}{}_{j} -\frac{i}{4}g^{3}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}L_c{}_{i}{}_{j}L_c{}^{k}{}^{l}\overline{\psi{}_{a}{}_{k}}\gamma{}^{a}{}^{b}\psi{}_{b}{}_{l}\\
&+\frac{3i}{2}g\widehat H{}^{a}{}^{b}\widehat H{}_{a}{}_{b}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\overline{\psi{}_{c}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{c}{}^{d}\psi{}_{d}{}_{j} -\frac{3i}{4}N_cg^{2}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\overline{\psi{}_{a}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{a}{}^{b}\psi{}_{b}{}_{j}\\
&-\frac{i}{4}g\big(\Box^c L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\big)\overline{\psi{}_{b}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}\psi{}_{c}{}_{j} +\frac{i}{24}C{}^{a}\widehat R(V){}^{b}{}^{c}{}^{i}{}^{j}\widehat G{}_{b}{}_{c}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}^{f}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{d}{}^{e}\psi{}^{g}{}_{j}
\end{aligned}
\tag{FL-EXPLICIT-23}
```

```math
\begin{aligned}
&+\frac{i}{6}C{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat R(V){}_{b}{}_{c}{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}^{f}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{d}{}^{e}\psi{}^{g}{}_{j} +\frac{i}{8}gC{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\big(\Box^c \sigma\big)\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}\overline{\psi{}^{d}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}\psi{}^{e}{}_{j}\\
&+\frac{i}{16}gC{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}\widehat G^2\overline{\psi{}^{d}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}\psi{}^{e}{}_{j} +\frac{i}{8}g^{3}C{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}L_c{}_{i}{}_{j}L_c{}^{k}{}^{l}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}\overline{\psi{}^{d}{}_{k}}\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}\psi{}^{e}{}_{l}\\
&-\frac{3i}{4}gC{}^{a}\widehat H{}^{b}{}^{c}\widehat H{}_{b}{}_{c}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}{}_{g}\overline{\psi{}^{f}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{d}{}^{e}\psi{}^{g}{}_{j} +\frac{3i}{8}N_cg^{2}C{}^{a}L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}\overline{\psi{}^{d}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}\psi{}^{e}{}_{j}\\
&+\frac{i}{8}gC{}^{a}\big(\Box^c L_c{}^{i}{}^{j}\big)\epsilon{}_{a}{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{f}\overline{\psi{}^{e}{}_{i}}\gamma{}^{c}{}^{d}\psi{}^{f}{}_{j}
\end{aligned}
\tag{FL-EXPLICIT-24}
```

### 11.3 Fermionic density and action

With $`\mathcal B`$ and $`\mathcal F_{\rm explicit}`$ given in full immediately above and $`\mathcal B|_0`$ given by (FL-31)–(FL-32) and the displayed bosonic components, the complete requested result is

```math
\boxed{e^{-1}\mathcal L_{\log,\rm ferm}=\left[\mathcal F_{\rm explicit}+\mathcal B-\mathcal B|_0\right]_{\le2}+\text{high fermi terms},\qquad S_{\log,\rm ferm}=\frac{\lambda_{\log}}{2\kappa_5^2}\int d^5x\,e\,\left[\mathcal F_{\rm explicit}+\mathcal B-\mathcal B|_0\right]_{\le2}+\text{high fermi terms}.}\tag{FL-FINAL}
```

Equivalently the full LOG BF density is $`\ell_{\log}=[\mathcal B+\mathcal F_{\rm explicit}]_{\le2}+\text{high fermi terms}`$. The subtraction in (FL-FINAL) isolates its fermionic part without discarding any supercovariant completion. This is an off-shell result through two physical fermions, exact in $`g`$ in the declared gauge. It retains supercovariant notation; it is not an expansion of all connection contributions into elementary fermion bilinears.
